{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"#!/usr/bin/env python3\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"MVP Gate Evaluation — compute 5 criteria, two sample groups.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport json\\nimport sys\\n\\n\\ndef load_results(path):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load results from JSON array or JSONL file.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        first_char = f.read(1)\\n        if not first_char:\\n            return []\\n        f.seek(0)\\n        if first_char == '[':\\n            return json.load(f)\\n        return [json.loads(line) for line in f if line.strip()]\\n\\n\\ndef evaluate(results, exclude_unknown=False):\\n    valid = [r for r in results if \\\"disagreement\\\" in r]\\n    if exclude_unknown:\\n        valid = [r for r in valid if r.get(\\\"ground_truth\\\") != \\\"Unknown\\\"]\\n\\n    total = len(valid)\\n\\n    effective = [\\n        r for r in valid\\n        if r.get(\\\"fol_exec\\\", {}).get(\\\"verdict\\\") not in (\\\"Error\\\", None)\\n        and r.get(\\\"code_exec\\\", {}).get(\\\"verdict\\\") not in (\\\"Error\\\", None)\\n    ]\\n    eff_n = len(effective)\\n\\n    # C1: disagree_rate among effective samples\\n    disagree = [r for r in effective if r[\\\"disagreement\\\"][\\\"agreement_type\\\"] == \\\"disagree\\\"]\\n    dis_n = len(disagree)\\n    c1_rate = dis_n / eff_n if eff_n else 0\\n\\n    # C2: diagnostic rate — at least one side correct among disagree\\n    if dis_n > 0:\\n        diag_n = sum(\\n            1 for r in disagree\\n            if r[\\\"disagreement\\\"].get(\\\"fol_correct\\\") or ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\nclass AgreementType(str, Enum):\\n    AGREE_CORRECT = \\\"agree_correct\\\"\\n    AGREE_WRONG   =    = \\\"exec_crash\\\"\\n    TIMEOUT            = \\\"timeout\\\"\\n    QUANTIFIER_ERROR   = \\\"quantifier_error\\\"\\n    NEGATION_ERROR     = \\\"negation_error\\\"\\n    CONSTRAINT_MISSING = \\\"constraint_missing\\\"\\n    CONSTRAINT_EXTRA   = \\\"constraint_extra\\\"\\n    TYPE_BOUNDARY      = \\\"type_boundary\\\"\\n    LOGIC_ERROR        = \\\"logic_error\\\"\\n    UNKNOWN            = \\\"unknown\\\"\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Result container\\n# / None\\n\\n    def to_dict(self):\\n        return asdict(self)\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Detection\\n# DisagreementResult:\\n\\n    fol_err  = (fol_verdict == \\\"Error\\\")\\n    code_err = (code_verdict == \\\"Error\\\")\\n\\n    if fol_err and code_err:\\n        return DisagreementResult(\\n            AgreementType.BOTH_ERROR, fol_verdict, code_verdict,\\n            ground_truth, None, None, ErrorType.EXEC_CRASH, \\\"both\\\",\\n        )\\n\\n    if fol_err or code_err:\\n        side = \\\"fol\\\" if fol_err else \\\"code\\\"\\n        return DisagreementResult(\\n            AgreementType.SINGLE_ERROR, fol_verdict, code_verdict,\\n            ground_truth,\\n            None if <details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I have a complete picture. Let me also check the `formalize.py` for `reset_usage` and `get_usage` to understand API tracking, and see if there are any `also_error` samples with `agree_wrong` type that need repair but don't have `error_side`.

From the pipeline code, the repair only happens when:
- `dis.agreement_type in (AgreementType.DISAGREE, AgreementType.SINGLE_ERROR)`
- And only on the sides indicated by `dis.error_side`

The data structure is:


- `rec["id"]` — sample ID
- `rec["premises"]`, `rec["conclusion"]`, `rec["ground_truth"]`
- `rec["fol_raw"]`, `rec["fol_code"]` — FOL generation results
- `rec["code_raw"]`, `rec["code_code"]` — Python code generation results
- `rec["fol_code_fixed"]` — (optional) syntax-fixed FOL code
- `rec["fol_exec"]` — `{output, error, verdict, timed_out}`
- `rec["code_exec"]` — same
- `rec["disagreement"]` — `{agreement_type, fol_verdict, code_verdict, ground_truth}`
- `rec["stability"]` — (optional) `{fol_verdict_2, code_verdict_2, fol_consistent, code_consistent, predicted_error_side}`
- `rec["repair"]` — `{fol_targeted: [...], fol_blind: [...], code_targeted: [...], code_blind: [...]}`
- `rec["api_usage"]` — `{cost_usd, ...}`

For the repair_only script, I need to merge the JSON and JSONL files while deduplicating by ID (with JSONL taking precedence), then for each sample that has an error_side in disagreement and is marked as DISAGREE or SINGLE_ERROR, I'll re-run both targeted repair and blind regeneration using the fixed versions.

The repair functions require premises, conclusion, original code, verdicts, error type, error message, representation type, and ground truth — all of which are already available in the record. I'm going to write the script now, importing from the existing repair and formalize modules to handle LLM call tracking, merging the data, re-running repairs where needed, and saving the results to a new output file.

Writing the repair script...
```

1. Merge JSON + JSONL data, dedup by `id` (JSONL wins)
2. For each sample that needs repair (agreement_type is DISAGREE or SINGLE_ERROR, and has error_side):
   - Extract existing FOL/code results from the record
   - Re-run repair using the fixed repair.py
   - Update the `repair` dict
   - Track API usage
3. Save complete results to new file

Key fields from existing records:


- `rec["fol_code"]` (or `rec.get("fol_code_fixed", rec["fol_code"])` for the actual executed FOL code)
- `rec["code_code"]`
- `rec["fol_exec"]["verdict"]`, `rec["fol_exec"]["error"]`
- `rec["code_exec"]["verdict"]`, `rec["code_exec"]["error"]`
- `rec["disagreement"]["agreement_type"]`, `rec["disagreement"]["error_side"]`, `rec["disagreement"]["error_type"]`
- `rec["ground_truth"]`
- `rec["premises"]`, `rec["conclusion"]`

{
  "server": "autodl-rtx6000pro",

调用）。\n\n输入：mvp_v4_full.json (JSON array) + mvp_v4_full.jsonl (JSONL, 可选)\n输出：mvp_v4_repair_fixed.json (完整 204 样本，repair 结果已更新)\n\n依赖：src/repair.py, src/pipeline.py (复用 repair/eval 函数)\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport argparse\nimport logging\nimport time\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))\n\nfrom formalize import reset_usage, get_usage\nfrom repair import targeted_repair, blind_regeneration\nfrom disagree import AgreementType\n\nlogging.basicConfig(\n    level=logging.INFO,\n    format=\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\",\n    handlers=[logging.StreamHandler()],\n)\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\ndef load_and_merge(json_path: str, jsonl_path: str = None) -> list:\n    records_by_id = {}\n\n    # Load JSON array\n    p = Path(json_path)\n    if p.exists():\n        with open(p) as f:\n            for rec in json.load(f):\n                records_by_id[rec[\"id\"]] = rec\n        log.info(\"loaded %d records from %s\", len(records_by_id), json_path)\n\n    # Load JSONL (overrides JSON records on conflict)\n    if jsonl_path:\n        jp = Path(jsonl_path)\n        if jp.exists():\n            jsonl_count = 0\n            override_count = 0\n            with open(jp) as f:\n                for line in f:\n                    line = line.strip()\n                    if not line:\n                        continue\n                    rec = json.loads(line)\n                    rid = rec[\"id\"]\n                    if rid in records_by_id:\n                        override_count += 1\n                    records_by_id[rid] = rec\n                    jsonl_count += 1\n            log.info(\"loaded %d records from %s (%d overrides)\", jsonl_count, jsonl_path, override_count)\n\n    results = sorted(records_by_id.values(), key=lambda r: r[\"id\"])\n    log.info(\"merged total: %d unique samples\", len(results))\n    return results\n\n\ndef needs_repair(rec: dict) -> bool:\n    dis = rec.get(\"disagreement\", {})\n    atype = dis.get(\"agreement_type\")\n    error_side = dis.get(\"error_side\")\n    return atype in (AgreementType.DISAGREE, AgreementType.SINGLE_ERROR) and error_side is not None\n\n\ndef repair_one(rec: dict) -> dict:\n    reset_usage()\n\n    dis = rec[\"disagreement\"]\n    error_side = dis[\"error_side\"]\n    et = dis.get(\"error_type\", \"unknown\")\n    gt = rec[\"ground_truth\"]\n    premises = rec[\"premises\"]\n    conclusion = rec[\"conclusion\"]\n\n    fol_code = rec.get(\"fol_code_fixed\", rec.get(\"fol_code\", \"\"))\n    code_code = rec.get(\"code_code\", \"\")\n    fol_verdict = rec.get(\"fol_exec\", {}).get(\"verdict\", \"Error\")\n    code_verdict = rec.get(\"code_exec\", {}).get(\"verdict\", \"Error\")\n    fol_error = rec.get(\"fol_exec\", {}).get(\"error\")\n    code_error = rec.get(\"code_exec\", {}).get(\"error\")\n\n    new_repair = {}\n\n    if error_side in (\"fol\", \"both\"):\n        log.info(\"[%s] targeted repair FOL …\", rec[\"id\"])\n        new_repair[\"fol_targeted\"] = [\n            r.to_dict() for r in targeted_repair(\n                premises, conclusion,\n                fol_code, fol_verdict, code_verdict,\n                et, fol_error, \"FOL (Z3)\", gt,\n            )\n        ]\n        log.info(\"[%s] blind regen FOL …\", rec[\"id\"])\n        new_repair[\"fol_blind\"] = [\n            r.to_dict() for r in blind_regeneration(\n                premises, conclusion,\n                \"FOL (Z3)\", gt,\n            )\n        ]\n\n    if error_side in (\"code\", \"both\"):\n        log.info(\"[%s] targeted repair Code …\", rec[\"id\"])\n        new_repair[\"code_targeted\"] = [\n            r.to_dict() for r in targeted_repair(\n                premises, conclusion,\n                code_code, code_verdict, fol_verdict,\n                et, code_error, \"Python code\", gt,\n            )\n        ]\n        log.info(\"[%s] blind regen Code …\", rec[\"id\"])\n        new_repair[\"code_blind\"] = [\n            r.to_dict() for r in blind_regeneration(\n                premises, conclusion,\n                \"Python code\", gt,\n            )\n        ]\n\n    rec[\"repair\"] = new_repair\n    rec[\"repair_rerun_usage\"] = get_usage()\n    return rec\n\n\ndef main():\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Repair-only rerun: skip generation, redo repair + eval\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--input\", required=True, help=\"JSON array input file\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--input-jsonl\", default=None, help=\"JSONL input file (optional, overrides JSON on conflict)\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--output\", required=True, help=\"Output JSON file\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--dry-run\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"Only load/merge data, don't call API\")\n    args = ap.parse_args()\n\n    records = load_and_merge(args.input, args.input_jsonl)\n\n    repair_targets = [r for r in records if needs_repair(r)]\n    skip_count = len(records) - len(repair_targets)\n    log.info(\"repair targets: %d, skip (no repair needed): %d\", len(repair_targets), skip_count)\n\n    if args.dry_run:\n        log.info(\"DRY RUN — not calling API. Would repair %d samples.\", len(repair_targets))\n        for r in repair_targets[:5]:\n            dis = r[\"disagreement\"]\n            log.info(\"  %s: %s, error_side=%s, error_type=%s\",\n                     r[\"id\"], dis[\"agreement_type\"], dis[\"error_side\"], dis.get(\"error_type\"))\n        if len(repair_targets) > 5:\n            log.info(\"  ... and %d more\", len(repair_targets) - 5)\n        return\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    for i, rec in enumerate(repair_targets, 1):\n        log.info(\"[%d/%d] repairing %s …\", i, len(repair_targets), rec[\"id\"])\n        try:\n            repair_one(rec)\n        except Exception as e:\n            log.error(\"[%s] repair error: %s\", rec[\"id\"], e)\n            rec[\"repair_rerun_error\"] = str(e)\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n\n    out_path = Path(args.output)\n    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    with open(out_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(records, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n\n    log.info(\"saved %d records to %s (%.1fs elapsed)\", len(records), args.output, elapsed)\n\n    # Quick eval summary\n    tgt_att = tgt_ok = bld_att = bld_ok = 0\n    for r in records:\n        rep = r.get(\"repair\", {})\n        for key in (\"fol_targeted\", \"code_targeted\"):\n            rounds = rep.get(key, [])\n            if rounds:\n                tgt_att += 1\n                if any(rd.get(\"success\") for rd in rounds):\n                    tgt_ok += 1\n        for key in (\"fol_blind\", \"code_blind\"):\n            rounds = rep.get(key, [])\n            if rounds:\n                bld_att += 1\n                if any(rd.get(\"success\") for rd in rounds):\n                    bld_ok += 1\n\n    tgt_rate = tgt_ok / tgt_att * 100 if tgt_att else 0\n    bld_rate = bld_ok / bld_att * 100 if bld_att else 0\n    log.info(\"C4 targeted: %d/%d (%.1f%%)  blind: %d/%d (%.1f%%)  delta: %+.1fpp\",\n             tgt_ok, tgt_att, tgt_rate, bld_ok, bld_att, bld_rate, tgt_rate - bld_rate)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\nSCRIPT_EOF\necho \"EXIT_CODE=$?\"",

}
